# SQX145 full-application reimplementation roadmap

Version: 3. Status: owner-approved documentation baseline; application allocations are proposals.
Inspected `2026-10-06T18:54:51.906614+00:00`; source HEAD `01067f00031428613c6394064ca1bcadc1ba00ee`; review state unreviewed.

- **Goal:** Implement the current donor's accepted behavior through phased host/workspace/plugin capabilities and connect the existing frontend.
- **Source:** `SQX_145_REFERENCE_ROOT` only. Download label 145-dev1; installed product build/activation and runtime parity unverified.
- **Current scope:** 289 JARs (123 libraries, 165 plugin archives, 1 runtime archive), 51,713 raw class entries, 17 immediate resource-only plugin directories, 49 named resource features, 366 phase tasks.
- **Authority:** `AGENTS.md`, project/architecture and owning domain READMEs. Donor informs; specification owns. No application feature registration or implementation is authorized by a roadmap alone.
- **JAR → feature / class-function → FR:** each archive has a proposed feature allocation. Exact declaration seeds below are independently testable research starting points, not confirmed functional semantics. Inspect all consumed classes/functions and bodies, including defaults, operation order, failure behavior and side effects.
- **UI:** use prebuilt views; each applicable feature includes current UI donors, frontend connection files and final connected acceptance. Mocks cannot qualify backend completion.
- **Missing bodies:** [current consumer gaps](evidence/p00-common-core.md). Do not fill gaps with unsupported algorithms.
- **Current research:** [all JAR references](../sqx/README.md), [baseline audit](sqx145-baseline-audit.md), [evidence procedure](evidence/README.md), [implementation prompts](sqx-implementation-prompts.md), [master checklist](phases/implementation_checklist.md).

## Completion contract

- Research actual current donor bodies and callers; record atomic observed/inferred/normative/unverified claims and explicit gaps.
- Ratify the owning FEAT/FR/DEC contract in the canonical implementation plan; implement only approved paths with `docs/templates/PYTHON_MODULE.md`, typed APIs and explicit requirement logs.
- Reuse/adapt current observable contracts; disposition JVM-only or unconsumed dependency internals explicitly. No guessed behavior or silent provider substitution.
- Test independent normal/boundary/failure vectors and applicable lifecycle/retained-data cases; wire the feature-owned backend to the prebuilt frontend.
- Record actual verification timestamps/artifacts; update the master checklist after each completed task. Complete P19 before P18 final qualification.
- Numerical/application parity remains unverified until independent execution proves it; static hashes, matching screens and coverage do not prove equivalence.
